## Self-review

What follows is anything in this plan that is **not grounded in code or documents I read**, and what was and was not run.

**Run.** Every code block in T1a to T11 was executed in a scratch repository outside this one, task by task: tests written first and seen to fail, the implementation added, and `make check` green at the end of each task (321 tests at the end, `mypy --strict` and ruff clean, 96% coverage). The staged tree was compared with the verified tree and is identical. `actionlint`, `zizmor --offline`, `shellcheck` and `shfmt` were run on the workflows and the script. The GYST changes in T12 were applied to a scratch copy of GYST `main` (its whole suite passes with them). Python 3.11 was not available here, so the suite ran on 3.13 only; ruff and mypy target 3.11.

**Not run.** Every step marked "not run": pushing, opening pull requests, `make venv` against the real hash locks (the locks were generated, and installed from, in the scratch venv), `scripts/apply-baseline.sh --apply`, the live audit, bootstrap and reconcile against real boards, tagging, and the GYST pull request's CI.

**Taken from memory or from the spec's lessons, not verified against GitHub, Codex, Claude Code, Copilot or Ollama.** The fakes model these, so a mistake here shows up on the first real run:

- GraphQL shapes: `createProjectV2Field` needing a colour and a description on every option, the `iterationConfiguration` input, `createProjectV2View`/`updateProjectV2View` and its `filter` (taken from the spec's lesson, not from the schema), `createProjectV2StatusUpdate`, `repositoryOwner`-free `organization`/`user` roots, `fieldValueByName` with `@include`, `items.totalCount`, and NOT_FOUND being the error type for a deleted node and for a repository the installation does not cover.
- Rate-limit detection: a 403 or 429 with `Retry-After`, or a body mentioning a secondary rate limit, or `x-ratelimit-remaining: 0`; anything else is a refusal.
- Codex: only `rate_limits` (`plan_type`, `primary`, `secondary`, `used_percent`, `resets_at`) is grounded (the prior-art `agents-status` reads it); `info.total_token_usage` and its key names are from memory.
- Claude Code transcripts: the row shape (`message.usage`, `message.id`, one row per content block), subagent transcripts living under the same `projects` tree, and the timestamp field are from memory.
- Copilot: that `gh agent-task list -L 200` prints an ISO date on each line (otherwise the count is reported as inexact), and the billing endpoint `/users/{login}/settings/billing/premium_request/usage` with `usageItems[].grossQuantity`. The `monthly_premium_requests: 300` allowance in `agents.yaml` is a placeholder to be replaced with the real plan's number.
- Ollama: `/api/tags`, `/api/ps` (`models[].name`) and a non-streaming `/api/generate` with a `think` flag and `eval_count`/`eval_duration` in the answer.
- The reviewers I assigned to shapes the coordinator did not specify (research, plan-writing, integration, well-specified code, adversarial review, the local shapes) and the Codex weekly threshold of 90% are my choices; the stated routes and the 80% and 70% thresholds are as given.

**Decisions that go beyond the spec's sketch** (each is optional in `board.yaml`, documented in `docs/board-yaml.md`): `rules.status.opened_pr` and `draft_pr` (GYST's separate pull-request and draft statuses), `rules.new_item_defaults`, `rules.add_closed_days` (the GYST project board's closed-in-30-days items), `repositories[].area_rules` (the title-keyword Area table in `board_reconcile.py`), an `agents:` section, an optional `project.number` (so `bootstrap` can create the project), and the Action accepting GYST's board-less inputs and both `client-id` and the deprecated `app-id`. When drift and an unanswerable question occur together, audit exits 2.

**Behaviour deliberately not ported.** `board_reconcile.py` flipped an open card in Done back to Backlog and classified Kind and Priority by title keywords; reconcile never reopens (GYST's rule) and audit reports it, and only a generic default (`new_item_defaults`) is kept. `fields_dump.py` is obsolete because ids are resolved live on every run, and `board_migrate.py` was a one-off migration, so neither is ported. Nothing GYST's `project_sync.py` did is lost: draft pull requests, `ready_for_review`/`converted_to_draft`, clearing the date on reopen, dry run without a token, the user-project refusal (now credential-based), one-request item lookup, validation before any mutation, and `reconcile: false`. Its per-repository reconcile scope is replaced by `repositories` (the wrapper builds a one-repository board from the calling repository).

**Statements to check.** `docs/usage.md` says `safo usage` sends nothing about sessions anywhere and names its two network paths (`gh`'s own calls, and probes of your own endpoints); "nothing leaves the machine" is not literally true for the Copilot read, so it does not say that. The privacy test cannot contain the employer's name, a callsign or regions, so those come from a file outside the repository. `docs/superpowers/plans/` is excluded from the privacy scan. `docs/lessons.md` dates the first-week lessons as a range because the notes give one range, not a date each. The GYST wrapper's `allowed-endpoints` default (PyPI added) is a first guess to be corrected from the CI fixture's egress report. SAFO's release uses `python-package-release.yml` at v1.14.0, which predates GYST's SBOM inputs, so v0.1.0 ships without an SBOM. The PR grouping and the T-numbering suffixes (T1a to T1c, T9a to T9g, T10a to T10c) are my splits of the requested tasks. The Opus attribution line is on the spec and plan commits as asked; the plan's own commit steps use a generic `Claude` line.
